'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		if EtiketNo mod BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1 then
			Ust = UstMargin
			if EtiketNo <> 1 then
				res = pe1.PageBreak()
			end if
		else
			Ust = UstMargin + ((EtiketNo -1) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi) * EtiketlerArasi
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar)
	Next
	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	End if
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
	D1 = solMargin + EtiketEni / 2
	D2 = solMargin + 3 * EtiketEni / 4
	Y1 = Ust + EtiketBoyu / 3
	Y2 = Ust + EtiketBoyu / 2
	Y3 = Ust + 2 * EtiketBoyu / 3

	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Alt - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, D2, Alt - Y1, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, solMargin, 0, D1 - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)

	sol = solmargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Ust
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",7)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"SUPPLIER / TEDARÝKÇÝ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",20)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 3, sol + 3,10,sag - sol - 3, "")

	BarkodNo = d7LB.KOD(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 8)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(BarkodNo)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 8, solMargin + 5, 13, BarkodGen, "CODE128", trim(BarkodNo), "")
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	sol = solMargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y2
'	CHStokKodu = Right("00000" & trim(d7LB.KOD(satirNo)), 12)
	CHStokKodu = trim(d7LB.SKOD(satirNo))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 0.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PART NUMBER /PARÇA NO")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",30)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4, sol,16,sag - sol,CHStokKodu)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, sol,4,sag - sol, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", d7LB.SKOD(satirNo), ""))


	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Ust
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",7)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"RECEIVER / ALICI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",16)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 3, sol,20,sag - sol, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", d7LB.CH_MUSTERI(satirNo), ""))

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "QUANTITY / MÝKTAR")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 36)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sol, 20, sag - sol - 2, Doc.NumFormat(KutuMiktari,15,0))

	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "WEIGHT / AÐIRLIK")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 30)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Agirlik = Stok00_ToUnit(d7LB.SKOD(satirNo), d7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo), "KG", -1) * KutuMiktari
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.Round(Agirlik,0) & " kg")

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y3 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "BATCH NO / LOT NO")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4, sol, 10, sag - sol, d7LB.SERINO(satirNo))

	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y3 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "DATE / TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4, sol, 10, sag - sol, Doc.StrToNorDate(d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo)))
End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function Stok00_ToUnit(StokKodu, EskiBirim, YeniBirim, Op)
	KonteynerAg = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_2", StokKodu, "")
	KonteynerAdet = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_1", StokKodu, "")
	Stok00_ToUnit = Doc.Div(KonteynerAg, KonteynerAdet)
End Function

Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, EtiketEni, d7LB
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	sayfaBoyu = 101
	sayfaEni = 200
	Ustmargin = 1
	EtiketBoyu = 48
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 50
	solMargin = 1
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 1
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 2

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "LANDSCAPE")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		if EtiketNo mod BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1 then
			Ust = UstMargin
			if EtiketNo <> 1 then
				res = pe1.PageBreak()
			end if
		else
			Ust = UstMargin + ((EtiketNo -1) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi) * EtiketlerArasi
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar)
	Next
	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	End if
End Sub

Sub PrintLabel()
	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	d7LB.GroupBy("KOD")
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 10
	EtiketBoyu = 48
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 55
	solMargin = 6
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 6
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 5

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		if EtiketNo mod BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1 then
			Ust = UstMargin
			if EtiketNo <> 1 then
				res = pe1.PageBreak()
			end if
		else
			Ust = UstMargin + ((EtiketNo -1) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi) * EtiketlerArasi
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar)
	Next
	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	End if
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : NET10 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETK-RNT-KS
Hangi Tablo için : STOK60
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Renault Küçük Kasa Etiketi - Net Cývata
Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, Adres, FirmaKodu, FirmaAdi, S60T
Sub PrintLabel()
	Set ff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	FirmaAdi ="NET CIVATA"
	FirmaKodu = "00115225"

	STOK60T.SafePack()
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2("dd4gf6", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI<>'E'", "", -1)
	for i = 1 to S60T.GetRecCount()
		stokKodu = S60T.KOD(i)
		KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
		KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,6)
		if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then
			Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " sandýk içi miktar girilmemiþ. (Stok Kartý Nümerik Taným - 1)", "", MB_OK
			Hata = "Hata"
		end if
		KasaNetAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_2",StokKodu,"")
		KasaNetAgirligi = Doc.Round(KasaNetAgirligi,6)
		if KasaNetAgirligi = 0 then
			Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " parça aðýrlýðý girilmemiþ. (Stok Kartý Nümerik Taným - 2)", "", MB_OK
		end if		
		KasaBrutAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_3",StokKodu,"")
		KasaBrutAgirligi = doc.Round(KasaBrutAgirligi,6)
		if KasaBrutAgirligi = 0 then
			Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " kasa aðýrlýðý girilmemiþ. (Stok Kartý Nümerik Taným - 3)", "", MB_OK
		end if		
	Next
	if trim(Hata) <> "" then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(Hata & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem tamamlanmadan kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 70
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 72
	solMargin = 6
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 6

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoChecksum(0)

	For i = 1 to S60T.GetRecCount()

		Miktar = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)	
		StokKodu = S60T.KOD(i)

		KasaIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
		KasaIciMiktar = doc.Round(KasaIciMiktar,0)
	
'		if KasaIciMiktar = 0 then KasaIciMiktar = Miktar
		'KasaIciMiktar = Miktar
		EtiketAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(Miktar, KasaIciMiktar))
		for j = 1 to EtiketAdedi
			Kalan = Miktar - (j-1) * KasaIciMiktar
			if Kalan < KasaIciMiktar then
				KutuMiktari = Kalan
			else
				KutuMiktari = KasaIciMiktar
			end if
'			for k = 1 to 2 'her etiketten 2 tane
			for k = 1 to 1
				EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
				if EtiketNo mod 4 = 1 then
					Ust = UstMargin
					if EtiketNo <> 1 then
						res = pe1.PageBreak()
					end if
				else
					Ust = UstMargin + ((EtiketNo - 1) mod 4) * EtiketlerArasi
				end if
				Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari)
			next
		Next
	Next

	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1, satirNo, KutuMiktari)
					
	StokKodu = S60T.KOD(satirNo)
	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
' dikey çizgilerin yerleri
	D0 = SolMargin + 70
	D1 = SolMargin + 105
	D2 = SolMargin + 125
	D3 = SolMargin + 130
	D4 = SolMargin + 153
	D5 = SolMargin + 158

'yatay çizgilerin yerleri
	Y1 = Ust + 7
	Y2 = Ust + 20
	Y3 = Ust + 28
	Y4 = Ust + 35.5
	Y5 = Ust + 43
	Y6 = Ust + 50
	Y7 = Ust + 57.5
	Y8 = Ust + 64
	Y9 = Ust + 70

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust + EtiketBoyu, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, D2, 0, D5 - D2)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, D2, 0, D5 - D2)
	res = pe1.Line(Y5, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y6, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y8, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)

	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D0, Y1 - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D3, Y1 - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, D1, Y2 - Y1, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D2, Y6 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D5, Y5 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y5, D1, Alt - Y5, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D4, Y8 - Y7, 0)

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D0
	rUst = Ust + 1
'	Alici1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "SHIPTOCODE", S60T.SIPNO(satirNo), "")
	Alici1 = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE
	Alici2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SHPT00J", "AD", Alici1, "")
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol,4,20,"ALICI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1, Sol + 5,10, sag - Sol - 5, trim(Alici2))

	sol = D0 + 1
	sag = D3
	rUst = Ust + 1
'	DockKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "ARTIKEL_REF_REF_3", S60T.SIPARTNO(satirNo), "ARTNO") 'LOC+159
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol + 1,6,15,"KAMYON ÝNDÝRME BÖLGESÝ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1, sol, 30, sag - sol, trim(Alici1))  

	sol = D3 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Ust + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol,4,20,"ÝMALATÇI")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 20)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 1, sol + 10, 20, sag - sol - 10, FirmaAdi)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Adres)


	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y1 + 1
'	IrsaliyeNo = right("00000000" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO),8)
'	RotaKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2", S60T.SIPARTNO(satirNo), "ARTNO")
	sol1 = Sol + 4
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol,16,10,"ROTA" & vbcrlf & "KODU")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
'	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
'	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
'	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(0,0,0))
'	res = pe1.SetTextColor(RGB(255,255,255))
'	res = pe1.Box(Y1, sol1, Y2 - Y1, sag - sol1 - 1)
'	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)
'	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,255,255))
'	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",46)
'	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 4.7, sol1,28,sag - sol1-1,RotaKodu)
'	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	Sol1 = Sol + 10
	DockKodu = STOK60E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol,10,10,"KAPI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",48)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst-4, Sol1,28, sag - Sol1, Trim(DockKodu) ) 'LOC+11  trim(Alici1)


	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y2
	CHStokKodu = trim(Doc.GetCHStokKodu(S60T.KOD(satirNo),STOK60E.CH_KODU))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(CHStokKodu)
'	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol,4,25,"PARÇA NO (P)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",48)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 3.5, sol1, 25,sag - sol1,CHStokKodu)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+13.5,sol1,9,BarkodGen,"CODE39","P" & CHStokKodu & "","")


	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = D5
	rUst = Y2 + 1
'	KasaNetAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_AGIRLIK",StokKodu,"") * S60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	KasaNetAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_2",StokKodu,"") '* S60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, 10, "NET AÐIRLIK (KG)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 0.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(KasaNetAgirligi,15,0))

	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = D5
	rUst = Y3 + 1
	DegisiklikTarihi = ""
'	KasaBrutAgirligi = GetBosKasaAgirligi(StokKodu,STOK60E.CH_KODU) + KasaNetAgirligi
	KasaBrutAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_3",StokKodu,"") '+ KasaNetAgirligi
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, 10, "BRÜT AÐIRLIK (KG)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 1, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(KasaBrutAgirligi,15,0))

	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = D5
	rUst = Y4 + 0.5
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, 12, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 24)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 1.5, sol + 1, 16, sag - sol, "D" & Replace(Mid(STOK60E.TARIH, 3),"/",""))

	sol = D5 + 0.5
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y2 + 0.5
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(KutuMiktari)
	sol1 = Sol + 5
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"MÝKTAR (Q)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",48)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 2, sol1,25, sag - sol1, KutuMiktari)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+15, sol1, 6, BarkodGen, "CODE39", "Q" & KutuMiktari & "","")

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y5 + 0.5
	sol1 = Sol + 10
'	SeriNo = Doc.SSCC0()
'	SeriNo = trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO) & "-" & EtiketNo

	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(SeriNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol,4,sag - sol,"ETÝKET NO (S)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",48)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 3.5, sol1,20,sag - sol1, SeriNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+13.5,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","S" & SeriNo,"")

	sol = D1 + 0.5
	sag = D2

	DegisiklikTarihi = ""

'	ParcaAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol,4,sag - sol - 1,"PARÇA TANIMI")

	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 2, sol + 10,15,sag - sol - 10,ParcaAdi)

	sol = D1 + 0.5

'	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(trim(S60T.KOD(satirNo)))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+1,pe1.GetRight + 1,5,BarkodGen,"CODE39",trim(S60T.KOD(satirNo)),"")

	End if

End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function GetKasaInfo(StokKodu, CHKodu, SevkAdresKodu)
	Dim KasaArray(1)
	KasaArray(0) = ""
	KasaArray(1) = 0

	QStr = "EVRAKNO='"+StokKodu
	if trim(CHKodu) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr + "'&CH_KODU='"+trim(CHKodu)
	End if
	if trim(SevkAdresKodu) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr + "'&SHIPTOCODE='"+trim(SevkAdresKodu)
	End if
	QStr = QStr + "'"
	Set S0A = Doc.Select1("S0AKASA", "STOK0A", QStr, "", -1)
	if S0A.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Set S0A = Doc.Select1("S0AKASA", "STOK0A", "EVRAKNO='"+StokKodu+"'&VARSAYILAN='E'", "", -1)
	End if
	
	KasaArray(0)=S0A.AMBALAJKODU(1)
	KasaArray(1)=S0A.SF_PAKETICERIGI(1)
	GetKasaInfo = KasaArray

	Doc.DeleteTableObject "S0AKASA"
End Function
Function GetBosKasaAgirligi(StokKodu,CHKodu)

	KasaArray = GetKasaInfo(StokKodu, CHKodu,"")
	if KasaArray(0) = "" Then 
		GetBosKasaAgirligi = 0
		Exit Function
	End if
	GetBosKasaAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_AGIRLIK", KasaArray(0), "")
End Function

Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, Adres, FirmaKodu, FirmaAdi, S60T
Sub PrintLabel()
	FirmaAdi = "NET CIVATA"
	FirmaKodu = "00115225"

	sET D = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	STOK60T.SafePack()
	for i = 1 to STOK60T.GetRecCount()
		stokKodu = STOK60T.KOD(i)
		Ambalaj = STOK60T.FATURASIZMI(i)
		if trim(Ambalaj) <> "E" then
			Set S00 = Doc.Select1("de344", "STOK00", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'", "", -1)
			KutuIciMiktar = S00.B_B_TECH_1
			KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,6)
			if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then
				Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " sandýk içi miktar girilmemiþ. (Stok Kartý Nümerik Taným - 1)", "", MB_OK
			end if
'			PaletBasinaKasaAdedi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_2",StokKodu,"")
'			PaletBasinaKasaAdedi = doc.Round(PaletBasinaKasaAdedi,6)
'			if PaletBasinaKasaAdedi = 0 then
'				Hata = Hata & i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " palet baþýna kasa adedi girilmemiþ. (Stok Kartý Nümerik Taným - 2)" & vbcrlf
'			end if		
			KasaNetAgirligi = S00.B_B_TECH_2
			KasaNetAgirligi = doc.Round(KasaNetAgirligi,6)
			if KasaNetAgirligi = 0 then
				Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " parça aðýrlýðý girilmemiþ. (Stok Kartý Nümerik Taným - 2)", "", MB_OK
			end if		
			KasaBrutAgirligi = S00.B_B_TECH_3
			KasaBrutAgirligi = doc.Round(KasaBrutAgirligi,6)
			if KasaBrutAgirligi = 0 then
				Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " kasa aðýrlýðý girilmemiþ. (Stok Kartý Nümerik Taným - 3)", "", MB_OK
			end if		
			if trim(STOK60T.NOTES(i)) = "" Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " palet numarasý girilmemiþ. (Ýrsaliyeye Çýkacak Özel Stok Açýklamasý)", "", MB_OK
				Hata = "1"	
			End if
		end if
	Next
	if trim(STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Sevk Adres Kodu seçilmelidir.", "", MB_OK
		Hata = "2"
	End if
	if trim(STOK60E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_2) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Sevk Adresleri ikinci ünvan alanýna gideceði yer yazýlmalýdýr (MONGE1 gibi..).", "", MB_OK
		Hata = "3"
	End if
	if trim(Hata) <> "" then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem tamamlanmadan kesilmiþtir.","",MB_OK
		exit sub
	end if

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 148
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 143
	solMargin = 6
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 6

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoChecksum(0)

	
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2("asda", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI<>'E'", "", -1)
'	S60T.GroupBy("NOTES")
	MSGBOX S60T.GetRecCount()
	For i = 1 to S60T.GetRecCount()
		for k = 1 to 1 'her etiketten 2 tane
			EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
'			if EtiketNo mod 2 = 1 then
				Ust = UstMargin
				if EtiketNo <> 1 then
					res = pe1.PageBreak()
				end if
'			else
'				Ust = UstMargin + EtiketlerArasi
'			end if
			Call Etiket(pe1,i)
		next
	Next

	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo)

	StokKodu = S60T.KOD(satirNo)
	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
' dikey çizgilerin yerleri
	D1 = SolMargin + 95
	D2 = D1 + 36
	D3 = D1 + 48
	D4 = D1 + 72

'yatay çizgilerin yerleri
	Y1 = Ust + 20
	Y2 = Y1 + 13
	Y3 = Y2 + 13
	Y4 = Y3 + 30
	Y5 = Y4 + 10.5
	Y6 = Y4 + 28
	Y7 = Y5 + 28
	Y8 = Y6 + 21
	Y9 = Y8 + 23

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Box(Ust, SolMargin, Y9 - Ust, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y5, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y6, SolMargin, 0, D1 - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y8, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)

	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Y3 - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D2, Y3 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D4, Y3 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, D1, Alt - Y4, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D3, Y8 - Y7, 0)

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Ust + 1
	Alici1 = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE
	Alici2 = STOK60E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_1
	Alici3 = STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, SolMargin + 2.5,4,20,"ALICI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 2, SolMargin + 12,18, sag - Sol, trim(Alici1))
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 9, SolMargin + 2,10, sag - Sol,trim(Alici2))
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 7, SolMargin + 2,8, sag - Sol,trim(Alici3))

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Ust + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol + 1,4,50,"KAPI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 52)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1, sol, 30, sag - sol, trim(STOK60E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_2))

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	IrsaliyeNo = right("00000000" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO),8)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(IrsaliyeNo)
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ÝRSALÝYE NO (N)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",52)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol + 2,28,sag - sol1,IrsaliyeNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
'	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+9,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","N" & trim(IrsaliyeNo) & "","")

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ÝMALATÇI")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sol, 20, sag - sol, FirmaAdi)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Adres)

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y3
	CHStokKodu = trim(Doc.GetCHStokKodu(S60T.KOD(satirNo),STOK60E.CH_KODU))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(CHStokKodu)
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PARÇA NO (P)")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",52)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(rUst - 2, sol1 + 10,25,sag - sol1 - 10,CHStokKodu)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+16,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","P" & CHStokKodu & "","")
	End if

	sol = D1
	sag = sagmargin
	rUst = Y4
'	ParcaAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")
	ParcaAdi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(S60T.KOD(satirNo),STOK60E.CH_KODU)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 1,4,sag - sol - 1,"PARÇA TANIMI")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",28)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 1,15,sag - sol - 1,ParcaAdi)
	End if

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D3
	rUst = Y7 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol + 1, 5, 12, "SEVK TARÝHÝ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 0.5, sol, 16, sag - sol, "D" & Replace(Mid(STOK60E.TARIH, 3),"/",""))

	sol = D3 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y7 + 1
	Revizyon = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_3",S60T.KOD(satirNo),""))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, 36, "ÝNDÝS NO")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
'		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.Getright, 15, sag - pe1.Getright, DegisiklikTarihi)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 0.5, sol, 16, sag - sol, Revizyon)
	End if
	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y4
	PaletMiktari = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(PaletMiktari)
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"MÝKTAR (Q)")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",52)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(rUst - 4, sol1,25, sag - sol1, PaletMiktari)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+14,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","Q" & PaletMiktari & "","")
	End if

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y2 + 1
	PaletNetAgirligi = GetNetAgirlik(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "NET AÐIRLIK (KG)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 0.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(PaletNetAgirligi,15,0))

	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = D4
	rUst = Y2 + 1
	PaletBrutAgirligi = GetBrutAgirlik(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "BRÜT AÐIRLIK (KG)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 0.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(PaletBrutAgirligi,15,0))

	sol = D4 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y2 + 1
	KasaAdedi = GetKasaAdedi(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "KUTU MÝKTARI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 0.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(KasaAdedi,15,0))


	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y5 + 1
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(StokKodu)
	sol1 = sol + 5
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, 30, "ÝMALATÇI PARÇA NO")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, sol1, 20, sag - sol1, trim(StokKodu))
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+11,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39",trim(StokKodu),"")

	sol = solmargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y6
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(FirmaKodu)
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ÝMALATÇI KODU (V)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1.5, sol1 + 10,10,sag - sol1, FirmaKodu)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+5,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","V" & FirmaKodu,"")

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y8
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		MonoGroup = "M"
	Else
		MonoGroup = "G"
	End if
	PaletNo = trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO) & Right("000" & trim(S60T.NOTES(satirNo)), 3)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(PaletNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ETÝKET NO (" & MonoGroup & ")")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 0.5, sol1 + 10,10,sag - sol1, PaletNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+8,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39",MonoGroup & PaletNo,"")

	sol = D1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y8
	PartiNo = trim(S60T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(PartiNo)
	sol1 = Sol + 5
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"LOT NUMARASI (H)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1.5, sol1 + 15,10,sag - sol1 - 15, PartiNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	if trim(PartiNo) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+6,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","H" & trim(PartiNo),"")
	End if


	GuvenlikParcasi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_5", S60T.KOD(satirNo), "")

		resim = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & "images\guvenlik.jpg"
		res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.Picture(Y3+2, sagMargin-35, 25, 25, resim, 114)
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	Elseif GuvenlikParcasi =2 Then
		resim = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & "images\guvenliks.jpg"
		res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.Picture(Y3+2, sagmargin-35, 25, 25, resim, 114)
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)	
	End if

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(Y9 - 4, sag - 32,5,32, "ODETTE Ver.1 Rev.4")
End Sub

Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function

Function GetNetAgirlik(satirNo)
	PaletNo = S60T.NOTES(satirNo)
	Set S60T2 = Doc.Select2("r345", "STOK60T", "NOTES='"+PaletNo+"'", "", -1)
	S60T2.GroupBy("KOD")
	for i = 1 to S60T2.GetRowCount()
		StokKodu = S60T2.KOD(i)
		NetAgirlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_2",StokKodu,"")
		KasaIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
		KasaAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(S60T2.SF_MIKTAR(i), KasaIciMiktar))
'		PaletNetAgirligi = PaletNetAgirligi + NetAgirlik '* S60T2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		PaletNetAgirligi = PaletNetAgirligi + NetAgirlik * KasaAdedi
	Next
	GetNetAgirlik = Doc.Round(PaletNetAgirligi, 0)
End Function

Function GetBrutAgirlik(satirNo)
	PaletNo = S60T.NOTES(satirNo)
	Set S60T2 = Doc.Select2("r345", "STOK60T", "NOTES='"+PaletNo+"'", "", -1)
	S60T2.GroupBy("KOD")
	for i = 1 to S60T2.GetRowCount()
		StokKodu = S60T2.KOD(i)
		BrutAgirlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_3",StokKodu,"")
		KasaIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
		KasaAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(S60T2.SF_MIKTAR(i), KasaIciMiktar))
'		PaletNetAgirligi = PaletNetAgirligi + NetAgirlik '* S60T2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		PaletBrutAgirligi = PaletBrutAgirligi + BrutAgirlik * KasaAdedi
	Next
	GetBrutAgirlik = Doc.Round(PaletBrutAgirligi, 0)
End Function

Function GetBrutAgirlik1(satirNo)
	NetAgirlik = GetNetAgirlik(satirNo)
	KasaAgirligi = GetKasaAgirligi(satirNo)
	GetBrutAgirlik = NetAgirlik + KasaAgirligi
End Function

Function GetKasaAgirligi(satirNo)
	PaletNo = S60T.BRUTAGIRLIK(satirNo)
	Set S60T2 = Doc.Select2("r345", "STOK60T", "BRUTAGIRLIK='"+PaletNo+"'", "", -1)
	S60T2.GroupBy("KOD")
	for i = 1 to S60T2.GetRowCount()
		StokKodu = S60T2.KOD(i)
		KasaAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_2",StokKodu,"")
		KasaIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
		KasaAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(S60T2.SF_MIKTAR(i), KasaIciMiktar))
		ToplamKasaAgirligi = ToplamKasaAgirligi + KasaAgirligi * KasaAdedi
	Next
	GetKasaAgirligi = Doc.Round(ToplamKasaAgirligi, 0)
End Function

Function GetKasaAdedi(satirNo)
	PaletNo = S60T.BRUTAGIRLIK(satirNo)
	Set S60T2 = Doc.Select2("r345", "STOK60T", "BRUTAGIRLIK='"+PaletNo+"'", "", -1)
	S60T2.GroupBy("KOD")
	for i = 1 to S60T2.GetRowCount()
		StokKodu = S60T2.KOD(i)
		KasaIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
		KasaAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(S60T2.SF_MIKTAR(i), KasaIciMiktar))
		ToplamKasaAdedi = ToplamKasaAdedi + KasaAdedi
	Next
	GetKasaAdedi = ToplamKasaAdedi
End Function

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : NET10 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETK-TOFAS
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Net Civata Tofaþ Sevk Etiketi - Küçük - Laser
Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, Adres, FirmaKodu, FirmaAdi, d7LB
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	FirmaAdi = "NET CIVATA A.Þ."
	FirmaKodu = "480"

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 10
	EtiketBoyu = 48
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 55
	solMargin = 6
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 6
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 5

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(i)
		KutuMiktari = Miktar
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		if EtiketNo mod BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1 then
			Ust = UstMargin
			if EtiketNo <> 1 then pe1.PageBreak()
		else
			Ust = UstMargin + ((EtiketNo -1) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi) * EtiketlerArasi
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari, EtiketAdedi)
	Next
	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	End if
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari, EtiketAdedi)
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
	D1 = solMargin + 102
	Y1 = Ust + 19
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Alt - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)

	sol = solmargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Ust
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(FirmaKodu)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"IMALATCI KODU (V)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst , sol,10,sag - sol, FirmaKodu)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+5,sol1,12,BarkodGen,"CODE39","V" & FirmaKodu,"")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 60, 10, sag - sol - 61, FirmaAdi)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5, pe1.GetLeft(), 10, sag - pe1.GetLeft, "LOT NO : " & trim(d7LB.SERINO(satirNo)))

	sol = solMargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y1
	CHStokKodu = Right("00000" & trim(d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)), 12)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(CHStokKodu)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 0.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PARÇA NO (P)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",40)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol,16,sag - sol,CHStokKodu)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+14,sol1,12,BarkodGen,"CODE39","P" & CHStokKodu & "","")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, sol,16,sag - sol, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", d7LB.SKOD(satirNo), ""))

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Ust
	SeriNo = 	trim(d7LB.KOD(satirNo))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(SeriNo)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol, 5, sag - sol - 2.5, "SERI NO (S)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol, 10, sag - sol, SeriNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+7,sol1,11,BarkodGen,"CODE39","S" & SeriNo & "","")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	Agirlik = Stok00_ToUnit(d7LB.SKOD(satirNo), d7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo), "KG", -1) * KutuMiktari
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sagMargin - 20, 10, 20, Doc.Round(Agirlik,0) & " kg")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop+7, sagMargin - 20, 10, 20, Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")

	sol = D1 + 1


	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(KutuMiktari)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen - 10) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "MÝKTAR (Q)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 36)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sol, 20, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(KutuMiktari,15,0))
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+14,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","Q" & KutuMiktari & "","")

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	AQP = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_6",StokKodu,"")
	if True then
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 18)
		res = pe1.Write(rUst + 21, sagMargin - 30, 20, 30, "AQP")
	end if

	sol = D1
	rUst = Y1
	sag = sagMargin
	Guvenlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_5",StokKodu,"")
	if Guvenlik = 1 then
		resim = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "images\guvenlik.jpg"
		res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.Picture(rUst + 5.5, sag - 20, 12, 12, resim, 114)
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	end if

End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function Stok00_ToUnit(StokKodu, EskiBirim, YeniBirim, Op)
	KonteynerAg = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_2", StokKodu, "")
	KonteynerAdet = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_1", StokKodu, "")
	Stok00_ToUnit = Doc.Div(KonteynerAg, KonteynerAdet)
End Function                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : NET10 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETK-YE1
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : YURTDIÞI ETÝKET - Laser
Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, EtiketEni, d7LB
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 10
	EtiketBoyu = 48
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 55
	solMargin = 6
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 6
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 5

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		if EtiketNo mod BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1 then
			Ust = UstMargin
			if EtiketNo <> 1 then
				res = pe1.PageBreak()
			end if
		else
			Ust = UstMargin + ((EtiketNo -1) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi) * EtiketlerArasi
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar)
	Next
	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	End if
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
	D1 = solMargin + EtiketEni / 2
	D2 = solMargin + 3 * EtiketEni / 4
	Y1 = Ust + EtiketBoyu / 3
	Y2 = Ust + EtiketBoyu / 2
	Y3 = Ust + 2 * EtiketBoyu / 3

	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Alt - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, D2, Alt - Y1, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, solMargin, 0, D1 - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)

	sol = solmargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Ust
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",7)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"SUPPLIER / TEDARÝKÇÝ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",20)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 3, sol + 3,10,sag - sol - 3, "NET CIVATA VE VÝDA SAN. A.Þ.")

	BarkodNo = d7LB.KOD(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 8)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(BarkodNo)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 8, solMargin + 5, 13, BarkodGen, "CODE128", trim(BarkodNo), "")
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	sol = solMargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y2
'	CHStokKodu = Right("00000" & trim(d7LB.KOD(satirNo)), 12)
	CHStokKodu = trim(d7LB.SKOD(satirNo))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 0.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PART NUMBER /PARÇA NO")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",30)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4, sol,16,sag - sol,CHStokKodu)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, sol,4,sag - sol, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", d7LB.SKOD(satirNo), ""))


	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Ust
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",7)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"RECEIVER / ALICI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",16)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 3, sol,20,sag - sol, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", d7LB.CH_MUSTERI(satirNo), ""))

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "QUANTITY / MÝKTAR")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 36)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sol, 20, sag - sol - 2, Doc.NumFormat(KutuMiktari,15,0))

	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "WEIGHT / AÐIRLIK")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 30)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Agirlik = Stok00_ToUnit(d7LB.SKOD(satirNo), d7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo), "KG", -1) * KutuMiktari
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.Round(Agirlik,0) & " kg")

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y3 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "BATCH NO / LOT NO")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4, sol, 10, sag - sol, d7LB.SERINO(satirNo))

	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y3 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "DATE / TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4, sol, 10, sag - sol, Doc.StrToNorDate(d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo)))
End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function Stok00_ToUnit(StokKodu, EskiBirim, YeniBirim, Op)
	KonteynerAg = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_2", StokKodu, "")
	KonteynerAdet = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_1", StokKodu, "")
	Stok00_ToUnit = Doc.Div(KonteynerAg, KonteynerAdet)
End Function

Sub PrintLabel()
	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	sayfaBoyu = 101
	sayfaEni = 200
	Ustmargin = 1
	EtiketBoyu = 48
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 50
	solMargin = 1
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 1
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 2

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "LANDSCAPE")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		if EtiketNo mod BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1 then
			Ust = UstMargin
			if EtiketNo <> 1 then
				res = pe1.PageBreak()
			end if
		else
			Ust = UstMargin + ((EtiketNo -1) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi) * EtiketlerArasi
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar)
	Next
	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	End if
End Sub

End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
	D1 = solMargin + EtiketEni / 2
	D2 = solMargin + 3 * EtiketEni / 4
	Y1 = Ust + EtiketBoyu / 3
	Y2 = Ust + EtiketBoyu / 2
	Y3 = Ust + 2 * EtiketBoyu / 3

	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Alt - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, D2, Alt - Y1, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, solMargin, 0, D1 - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)

	sol = solmargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Ust
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",7)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"SUPPLIER / TEDARÝKÇÝ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",20)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 3, sol + 3,10,sag - sol - 3, "NET CIVATA VE VÝDA SAN. A.Þ.")

	BarkodNo = d7LB.KOD(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 8)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(BarkodNo)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 8, solMargin + 5, 13, BarkodGen, "CODE128", trim(BarkodNo), "")
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	sol = solMargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y2
'	CHStokKodu = Right("00000" & trim(d7LB.KOD(satirNo)), 12)
	CHStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(d7LB.SKOD(satirNo), d7LB.CH_MUSTERI(satirNo))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 0.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PART NUMBER /PARÇA NO")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",20)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4, sol,16,sag - sol,CHStokKodu)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, sol,4,sag - sol, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", d7LB.SKOD(satirNo), ""))

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Ust
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",7)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"RECEIVER / ALICI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",16)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 3, sol,20,sag - sol, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", d7LB.CH_MUSTERI(satirNo), ""))

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "QUANTITY / MÝKTAR")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 36)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sol, 20, sag - sol - 2, Doc.NumFormat(KutuMiktari,15,0))

	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "WEIGHT / AÐIRLIK")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 30)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Agirlik = Stok00_ToUnit(d7LB.SKOD(satirNo), d7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo), "KG", -1) * KutuMiktari
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.Round(Agirlik,0) & " kg")

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y3 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "BATCH NO / LOT NO")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4, sol, 10, sag - sol, d7LB.SERINO(satirNo))

	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y3 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "DATE / TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4, sol, 10, sag - sol, Doc.StrToNorDate(d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo)))
End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function Stok00_ToUnit(StokKodu, EskiBirim, YeniBirim, Op)
	KonteynerAg = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_2", StokKodu, "")
	KonteynerAdet = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_1", StokKodu, "")
	Stok00_ToUnit = Doc.Div(KonteynerAg, KonteynerAdet)
End Function

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : NET10 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETK-YE4
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : YURTDIÞI ETÝKET - BÖLLHOFF (Müþteri Kodlu) Barkod Yazýcý Ýçin
Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, EtiketEni, d7LB
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	sayfaBoyu = 101
	sayfaEni = 200
	Ustmargin = 1
	EtiketBoyu = 48
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 50
	solMargin = 1
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 1
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 2

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "LANDSCAPE")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		if EtiketNo mod BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1 then
			Ust = UstMargin
			if EtiketNo <> 1 then
				res = pe1.PageBreak()
			end if
		else
			Ust = UstMargin + ((EtiketNo -1) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi) * EtiketlerArasi
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar)
	Next
	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	End if
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
	D1 = solMargin + EtiketEni / 2
	D2 = solMargin + 3 * EtiketEni / 4
	Y1 = Ust + EtiketBoyu / 3
	Y2 = Ust + EtiketBoyu / 2
	Y3 = Ust + 2 * EtiketBoyu / 3

	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Alt - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, D2, Alt - Y1, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, solMargin, 0, D1 - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)

	sol = solmargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Ust
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",7)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"SUPPLIER / TEDARÝKÇÝ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",20)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 3, sol + 3,10,sag - sol - 3, "NET CIVATA VE VÝDA SAN. A.Þ.")

	BarkodNo = d7LB.KOD(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 8)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(BarkodNo)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 8, solMargin + 5, 13, BarkodGen, "CODE128", trim(BarkodNo), "")
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	sol = solMargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y2
'	CHStokKodu = Right("00000" & trim(d7LB.KOD(satirNo)), 12)
	CHStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(d7LB.SKOD(satirNo), d7LB.CH_MUSTERI(satirNo))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 0.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PART NUMBER /PARÇA NO")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",20)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4, sol,16,sag - sol,CHStokKodu)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, sol,4,sag - sol, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", d7LB.SKOD(satirNo), ""))


	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Ust
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",7)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"RECEIVER / ALICI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",16)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 3, sol,20,sag - sol, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", d7LB.CH_MUSTERI(satirNo), ""))

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "QUANTITY / MÝKTAR")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 36)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sol, 20, sag - sol - 2, Doc.NumFormat(KutuMiktari,15,0))

	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "WEIGHT / AÐIRLIK")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 30)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Agirlik = Stok00_ToUnit(d7LB.SKOD(satirNo), d7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo), "KG", -1) * KutuMiktari
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.Round(Agirlik,0) & " kg")

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y3 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "BATCH NO / LOT NO")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4, sol, 10, sag - sol, d7LB.SERINO(satirNo))

	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y3 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "DATE / TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4, sol, 10, sag - sol, Doc.StrToNorDate(d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo)))
End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function Stok00_ToUnit(StokKodu, EskiBirim, YeniBirim, Op)
	KonteynerAg = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_2", StokKodu, "")
	KonteynerAdet = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_1", StokKodu, "")
	Stok00_ToUnit = Doc.Div(KonteynerAg, KonteynerAdet)
End Function                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : NET10 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETÝKET
Hangi Tablo için : STOK60
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Net Civata Tofaþ Sevk Etiketi - Küçük
Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, Adres, FirmaKodu, FirmaAdi

Sub PrintLabel()
	FirmaAdi = "NET CIVATA A.Þ."
	FirmaKodu = "480"

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 10
	EtiketBoyu = 48
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 55
	solMargin = 6
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 6
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 5

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)

	For i = 1 to STOK60T.GetRecCount()
		if trim(STOK60T.KOD(i)) <> "" and trim(STOK60T.FATURASIZMI(i)) <> "E" then
			Miktar = STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)	
			StokKodu = STOK60T.KOD(i)
			KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
			KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)
			if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = Miktar
			EtiketAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Miktar / KutuIciMiktar)
			for j = 1 to EtiketAdedi
				Kalan = Miktar - (j-1) * KutuIciMiktar
				if Kalan < KutuIciMiktar then
					KutuMiktari = Kalan
				else
					KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar
				end if
				EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
				if EtiketNo mod BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1 then
					Ust = UstMargin
					if EtiketNo <> 1 then
						res = pe1.PageBreak()
					end if
				else
					Ust = UstMargin + ((EtiketNo -1) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi) * EtiketlerArasi
				end if
				Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari, EtiketAdedi)
			Next
		end if
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari, EtiketAdedi)
	StokKodu = STOK60T.KOD(satirNo)
	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
	D1 = solMargin + 102
	Y1 = Ust + 19
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Alt - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)

	sol = solmargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Ust
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(FirmaKodu)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"IMALATCI KODU (V)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst , sol,10,sag - sol, FirmaKodu)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+5,sol1,12,BarkodGen,"CODE39","V" & FirmaKodu,"")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 60, 10, sag - sol - 61, FirmaAdi)

	sol = solMargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y1
	CHStokKodu = Right("00000" & trim(STOK60T.KOD(satirNo)), 12)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(CHStokKodu)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 0.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PARÇA NO (P)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",40)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol,16,sag - sol,CHStokKodu)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+14,sol1,12,BarkodGen,"CODE39","P" & CHStokKodu & "","")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, sol,16,sag - sol, STOK60T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo))

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Ust
	SeriNo = 	trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO) & "-" & trim(STOK60T.SERINO(satirNo))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(SeriNo)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol, 5, sag - sol - 2.5, "SERI NO (S)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol, 10, sag - sol, SeriNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+7,sol1,11,BarkodGen,"CODE39","S" & SeriNo & "","")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sagMargin - 20, 10, 20, trim(STOK60T.BRUTAGIRLIK(satirNo)) & " kg")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop+7, sagMargin - 20, 10, 20, Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")

	sol = D1 + 1


	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(KutuMiktari)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen - 10) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "MÝKTAR (Q)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 40)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sol, 20, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(KutuMiktari,15,0))
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+14,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","Q" & KutuMiktari & "","")

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	AQP = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_6",StokKodu,"")
	if True then
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 18)
		res = pe1.Write(rUst + 21, sagMargin - 30, 20, 30, "AQP")
	end if

	sol = D1
	rUst = Y1
	sag = sagMargin
	Guvenlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_6",StokKodu,"")
	if Guvenlik = 1 then
		resim = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "images\guvenlik.jpg"
		res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.Picture(rUst + 5.5, sag - 20, 12, 12, resim, 114)
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	end if

End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function

Form Kaynak Kodu

Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, Adres, FirmaKodu, FirmaAdi

Sub PrintLabel()
	FirmaAdi = "KORMETAL A.Þ."
	FirmaKodu = "480"

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 10
	EtiketBoyu = 48
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 55
	solMargin = 6
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 6
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 5

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)

	For i = 1 to STOK60T.GetRecCount()
		if trim(STOK60T.KOD(i)) <> "" and trim(STOK60T.FATURASIZMI(i)) <> "E" then
			Miktar = STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)	
			StokKodu = STOK60T.KOD(i)
			KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
			KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)
			if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = Miktar
			EtiketAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Miktar / KutuIciMiktar)
			for j = 1 to EtiketAdedi
				Kalan = Miktar - (j-1) * KutuIciMiktar
				if Kalan < KutuIciMiktar then
					KutuMiktari = Kalan
				else
					KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar
				end if
				EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
				if EtiketNo mod BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1 then
					Ust = UstMargin
					if EtiketNo <> 1 then
						res = pe1.PageBreak()
					end if
				else
					Ust = UstMargin + ((EtiketNo -1) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi) * EtiketlerArasi
				end if
				Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari, EtiketAdedi)
			Next
		end if
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari, EtiketAdedi)
	StokKodu = STOK60T.KOD(satirNo)
	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
	D1 = solMargin + 102
	Y1 = Ust + 19
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Alt - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)

	sol = solmargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Ust
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(FirmaKodu)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"IMALATCI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",36)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 3, sol,20,sag - sol, FirmaAdi)

	sol = solMargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y1
	CHStokKodu = trim(STOK60T.KOD(satirNo))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(CHStokKodu)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 0.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PARÇA NO (P)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",32)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 2, sol,16,sag - sol,CHStokKodu)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+14,sol1,12,BarkodGen,"CODE39","P" & CHStokKodu & "","")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, sol,16,sag - sol, STOK60T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo))

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Ust
	SeriNo = 	trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO) & "-" & trim(STOK60T.SERINO(satirNo))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(SeriNo)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol, 5, sag - sol - 2.5, "SERI NO (S)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol, 10, sag - sol, SeriNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+7,sol1,11,BarkodGen,"CODE39","S" & SeriNo & "","")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sagMargin - 20, 10, 20, trim(STOK60T.BRUTAGIRLIK(satirNo)) & " kg")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop+7, sagMargin - 20, 10, 20, Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")

	sol = D1 + 1


	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(KutuMiktari)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen - 10) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "MÝKTAR (Q)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 40)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sol, 20, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(KutuMiktari,15,0))
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+14,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","Q" & KutuMiktari & "","")

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	AQP = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_6",StokKodu,"")
	if True then
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 18)
		res = pe1.Write(rUst + 21, sagMargin - 30, 20, 30, "AQP")
	end if

	sol = D1
	rUst = Y1
	sag = sagMargin
	Guvenlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_6",StokKodu,"")
	if Guvenlik = 1 then
		resim = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "images\guvenlik.jpg"
		res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.Picture(rUst + 5.5, sag - 20, 12, 12, resim, 114)
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	end if

End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : NET10 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETÝKET-3
Hangi Tablo için : STOK60
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Net Civata Tofaþ Sevk Etiketi - Küçük (Yeni Barkod Printer Ýçin)
Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, Adres, FirmaKodu, FirmaAdi

Sub PrintLabel()
	FirmaAdi = "NET CIVATA A.Þ."
	FirmaKodu = "480"

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 101
	sayfaEni = 200
	Ustmargin = 1
	EtiketBoyu = 48
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 50
	solMargin = 1
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 1
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 2

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO), sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "LANDSCAPE")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)

	For i = 1 to STOK60T.GetRecCount()
		if trim(STOK60T.KOD(i)) <> "" and trim(STOK60T.FATURASIZMI(i)) <> "E" then
			Miktar = STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)	
			StokKodu = STOK60T.KOD(i)
			KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
			KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)
			if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = Miktar
			EtiketAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Miktar / KutuIciMiktar)
			for j = 1 to EtiketAdedi
				Kalan = Miktar - (j-1) * KutuIciMiktar
				if Kalan < KutuIciMiktar then
					KutuMiktari = Kalan
				else
					KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar
				end if
				EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
				if EtiketNo mod BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1 then
					Ust = UstMargin
					if EtiketNo <> 1 then
						res = pe1.PageBreak()
					end if
				else
					Ust = UstMargin + ((EtiketNo -1) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi) * EtiketlerArasi
				end if
				Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari, EtiketAdedi)
			Next
		end if
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari, EtiketAdedi)
	StokKodu = STOK60T.KOD(satirNo)
	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
	D1 = solMargin + 102
	Y1 = Ust + 19
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Alt - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)

	sol = solmargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Ust
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(FirmaKodu)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"IMALATCI KODU (V)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst , sol,10,sag - sol, FirmaKodu)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+5,sol1,12,BarkodGen,"CODE39","V" & FirmaKodu,"")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 60, 10, sag - sol - 61, FirmaAdi)

	sol = solMargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y1
	CHStokKodu = Right("00000" & trim(STOK60T.KOD(satirNo)), 12)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(CHStokKodu)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 0.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PARÇA NO (P)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",40)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol,16,sag - sol,CHStokKodu)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+14,sol1,12,BarkodGen,"CODE39","P" & CHStokKodu & "","")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, sol,4,sag - sol, STOK60T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo))

	sol = D1 + 1

	rUst = Ust

	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(SeriNo)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol, 5, sag - sol - 2.5, "SERI NO (S)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol, 10, sag - sol, SeriNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+7,sol1,11,BarkodGen,"CODE39","S" & SeriNo & "","")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 0.5, sagMargin - 20, 10, 20, trim(STOK60T.BRUTAGIRLIK(satirNo)) & " kg")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop+3, sagMargin - 20, 10, 20, Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(KutuMiktari)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen - 10) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 5)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "MÝKTAR (Q)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 40)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sol, 20, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(KutuMiktari,15,0))
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+14,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","Q" & KutuMiktari & "","")

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	AQP = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_6",StokKodu,"")

		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 18)

	end if

	sol = D1
	rUst = Y1
	sag = sagMargin
	Guvenlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_6",StokKodu,"")
	if Guvenlik = 1 then

		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.Picture(rUst + 5.5, sag - 20, 12, 12, resim, 114)
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	end if

----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : NET10 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : FORD-ETK
Hangi Tablo için : STOK60
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : FORD SEVKÝYAT ETÝKETÝ - A4
Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, S60T , S60E, Lokasyon, UreticiKodu
Sub PrintLabel()
	UreticiKodu = "EDWEA"

	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK60T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set S60 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	Set S60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	S60T.CopyTable(S60)
	S60T.GroupBy2("KOD")
	S60T.SafePack

	Hata = ""
	for i = 1 to S60T.GetRecCount()
		stokKodu = S60T.KOD(i)
		Ambalaj = S60T.FATURASIZMI(i)
		if trim(stokKodu) <> "" and trim(Ambalaj) <> "E" then
			SevkAdresKodu = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE
			if InStr(1, SevkAdresKodu, "D1D4F") > 0 Then Lokasyon = GetFordLokasyon(StokKodu)
			KonteynerKodu = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
			if trim(KonteynerKodu) = "" then
				Hata = Hata & i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " konteyner kodu girilmemiþ." & vbcrlf
			end if
'**********
				'KontSayisi = S60T.BRUTAGIRLIK(i)
				if trim(cstr(KontSayisi)) = "" or trim(cstr(KontSayisi)) = "0" then
					KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
					KutuIciMiktar = Doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)
				else
					ParcaKontSayisi = Doc.CDbl(KontSayisi)
					Miktar = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
					KutuIciMiktar = Doc.Div(Miktar,ParcaKontSayisi)
					KutuIciMiktar = Doc.Ceil(KutuIciMiktar)
				end if
'**********
			if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then
				Hata = Hata & i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " sandýk içi miktar girilmemiþ." & vbcrlf
			end if
		end if
	Next
	if trim(Hata) <> "" then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(Hata & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem tamamlanmadan kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	SevkAdresKodu = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE
	if trim(SevkAdresKodu) = "" then
		k = Doc.d7msgBox("Sevk adres kodu girilmemiþ","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 18
	EtiketBoyu = 117
	EtiketEni = 171
	EtiketlerArasi = 143
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Etiket" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO),sayfaBoyu,sayfaEni,"")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)

	solMargin = 20
	sagMargin = sayfaEni- 19
	For i = 1 to S60T.GetRecCount()
		Ambalaj = S60T.FATURASIZMI(i)
		if trim(S60T.KOD(i)) <> "" and trim(Ambalaj) <> "E" then
			Miktar = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)	
			StokKodu = S60T.KOD(i)
'			KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
'			KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)
'**********
				'KontSayisi = S60T.BRUTAGIRLIK(i)
				if trim(cstr(KontSayisi)) = "" or trim(cstr(KontSayisi)) = "0" then
					KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
					KutuIciMiktar = Doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)
				else
					ParcaKontSayisi = Doc.CDbl(KontSayisi)
					KutuIciMiktar = Doc.Div(Miktar,ParcaKontSayisi)
					KutuIciMiktar = Doc.Ceil(KutuIciMiktar)
				end if
'**********
			if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = Miktar
			EtiketAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Miktar / KutuIciMiktar)
			for j = 1 to EtiketAdedi
				Kalan = Miktar - (j-1) * KutuIciMiktar
				if Kalan < KutuIciMiktar then
					KutuMiktari = Kalan
				else
					KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar
				end if

'/*
'				HerEtikettenKacTane = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_3", StokKodu, "")
				if HerEtikettenKacTane = 0 Then HerEtikettenKacTane = 1
'				for k = 1 to HerEtikettenKacTane
				for k = 1 to 1
					EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
					if EtiketNo mod 2 = 1 then
						Ust = UstMargin
						if EtiketNo <> 1 then
							res = pe1.PageBreak()
						end if
					else
						Ust = UstMargin + EtiketlerArasi
					end if
					Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari)
				next

'*/
'				EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
'				if EtiketNo mod 2 = 1 then
'					Ust = UstMargin
'					if EtiketNo <> 1 then
'						res = pe1.PageBreak()
'					end if
'				else
'					Ust = UstMargin + EtiketlerArasi
'				end if
'				Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari)

			Next
		end if
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo, KutuMiktari)
' dikey çizgilerin yerleri
	D1 = SolMargin + 42
	D2 = SolMargin + 79
	D3 = SolMargin + 110
	D4 = SolMargin + 113
	D5 = SolMargin + 125
	D6 = SolMargin + 143

'yatay çizgilerin yerleri
	Y1 = Ust + 12.5
	Y2 = Ust + 24
	Y3 = Ust + 35
	Y4 = Ust + 45.5
	Y5 = Ust + 56
	Y6 = Ust + 66
	Y7 = Ust + 79
	Y8 = Ust + 88
	Y9 = Ust + 98
	Y10 = Ust + 117

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Box(Ust,SolMargin, EtiketBoyu, sagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1,SolMargin,0,sagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2,D2,0,sagMargin-D2)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3,SolMargin,0,sagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4,D5,0,sagMargin-D5)
	res = pe1.Line(Y5,SolMargin,0,sagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y6,D2,0,sagMargin-D2)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7,SolMargin,0,D2 - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y8,D2,0,sagMargin-D2)
	res = pe1.Line(Y9,SolMargin,0,sagMargin-SolMargin)

	res = pe1.Line(Y5,D1,Y9-Y5,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust,D2,Y3-Ust,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y5,D2,Y10-Y5,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2,D3,Y3-Y2,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y8,D4,Y9-Y8,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust,D5,Y1-Ust,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3,D5,Y5-Y3,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2,D6,Y3-Y2,0)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	CHKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
	SevkAdresKodu = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE
	if InStr(1, SevkAdresKodu, "D1D4F") > 0 Then
		Alici = "D1D4F - KOCAELI"
	Elseif InStr(1, SevkAdresKodu, "D1D4T") > 0 Then
		Alici = "D1D4T - ÝNÖNÜ KAMYON"
	Elseif InStr(1, SevkAdresKodu, "AUF9A") > 0 Then
		Alici = "AUF9A - KARTAL YP DEPO"
	Elseif InStr(1, SevkAdresKodu, "CVR1B") > 0 Then
		Alici = "CVR1B - KARTAL YP DEPO"
	Elseif InStr(1, SevkAdresKodu, "D1D4Y") > 0 Then
		Alici = "D1D4Y - KOCAELÝ PARÇA FB."
'	Else
'		k = doc.d7MsgBox("Sevk Adresi Tanýmlý Deðil", "Hata", MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING)
'		Exit Sub
	End if

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
